--- SUITE 4 ATTEMPT 1 OUTCOME (kept verbatim above; correction recorded here) ---
Attempt 1 (20:34-20:36) FAILED at PHASE B: prepare_data.py raised
soundfile.LibsndfileError opening '.work-finetune/regression/utt_zh01.wav'.
ROOT CAUSE (driver bug in this regression's scratch driver, NOT a product bug):
the driver passed --output-dir as a RELATIVE path; scripts/make_finetune_dataset.py
stores jsonl audio/ref paths verbatim from --output-dir (its default is ABSOLUTE,
built from _REPO_ROOT — which is why the 2026-09-20 Task 4 run worked), so the
jsonl carried repo-relative paths that prepare_data.py (cwd =
.upstream/Qwen3-TTS/finetuning) could not resolve. Phases C/D were cascade
failures of the same missing file (train: FileNotFoundError; reload: checkpoint
never written -> loader KeyError 'unknown model reference').
State after attempt 1: clone restored pristine by the driver (porcelain 0 lines,
HEAD 022e286, flash_attention_2 back at sft_12hz.py:51 — recorded above).
PHASE A itself was SUCCESSFUL (13 official renders, exit 0, 76 MIOpen lines
filtered) and is re-run below unchanged-in-method with the corrected
(absolute) --output-dir. Driver fixed: .work-finetune/regression_driver.sh now
passes --output-dir "$OUT" (absolute). Attempt 2 follows.
